# Global Central Bank Gold Activity Log

**Summary**: Append-only running log of all country-level central bank gold purchases, sales, and gold-related policy announcements worldwide. Updated daily by the automated routine. **Aug 28**: full sweep (4 searches + all named-entity checks) found no genuinely fresh (24-48h) country-level gold news — a quiet day for official-sector activity, with market attention on Warsh's Jackson Hole keynote instead; no new stub pages.

**Sources**: Web research, WGC monthly data, IMF IFS, Reuters, Bloomberg, central bank press releases

**Last updated**: 2026-08-28

---

## How This Page Works

- Each entry covers **one country** and **one event** (purchase, sale, policy, announcement)
- Entries are added daily by the automated routine when new country-level gold news is found
- If a country already has a dedicated wiki page, this log links to it and the dedicated page is updated too
- If a country does **not** yet have a dedicated page, the event is captured here until enough data warrants one

This is the 20th consecutive month the PBoC has increased its official gold stockpile. Total holdings now at **~2,346t**. China's year-to-date 2026 accumulation: ~40t (Jan–June). The June purchase at prices near $4,000–$4,100 confirms price-insensitive buying, consistent with structural de-dollarisation policy.
